# MedLuxLife — ChatGPT prompts, 4 images per card

Each card has a ready prompt for 4 different photos (1 = still life, 2 = people, 3 = everyday life / another idea, 4 = process / detail / place). Paste one prompt into ChatGPT, choose the best image and paste it into the Claude chat with its code.


## Medical Aesthetics

### m872 — Intro photo

```
Create FOUR separate photorealistic photos for a premium German medical-travel website (MedLuxLife), topic "Medical Aesthetics" (Medical Aesthetics). Each image must be its OWN separate file, NOT a grid or collage, and the four images must show four clearly different ideas, people, settings, light and colours. Name them m872-1.png, m872-2.png, m872-3.png, m872-4.png.

IMAGE 1 – still life, no people: Still life, no people. On a white limestone window ledge sits a shallow teal-glazed ceramic dish. In it rests one smooth, water-polished white pebble beaded with fresh droplets, beside a glossy, softly swirled swatch of clear hydrating gel. A single white ranunculus lies across the dish rim, petals just opening. Soft overcast daylight from a north window wraps everything evenly, with gentle shadows and a cool sheen on the gel. 45-degree angle, 90mm, shallow focus on the pebble. Calm, fresh, understated.

IMAGE 2 – people: Medium shot, 50mm, eye level, both faces in view. A Turkish woman in her 50s with cropped salt-and-pepper curls, olive skin, fine lines, thick dark brows, pearl studs and a white cotton shirt sits on the edge of an oak treatment couch, laughing. Standing at her shoulder, a Brazilian practitioner in his 30s, curly dark top-knot and forest-green scrubs, smiles as he fastens a soft white terry headband over her curls, his hands at her crown, away from her face. Half-drawn forest-green linen curtain behind, warm late-morning side sun. Light-hearted, relaxed.

IMAGE 3 – everyday life / another idea: Medium shot, waist up, of a Scandinavian woman in her 20s at an outdoor flower stall on an overcast morning. She has a very short platinum-blonde crop, pale freckled skin, small silver hoops, an ivory wool coat and a blush silk scarf. Laughing naturally, she holds a loose bunch of blush ranunculus against her shoulder. Zinc buckets of ivory and pale-pink peonies fill the blurred foreground and background under a plain ivory canvas awning, with no signs or price tags. Soft, even overcast light. Light, unhurried city mood.

IMAGE 4 – process, detail or place: Medium shot of a quiet aesthetic treatment room in the evening, no faces. Charcoal lime-plaster walls, a padded treatment couch in charcoal leather with a crisp folded white towel. A brass articulated magnifier lamp and a brass floor lamp cast warm pools of light; the window beyond is dark. On a small brass-framed trolley sit a covered steel tray and a stack of white cotton pads. At the frame edge, a practitioner's hand in a charcoal scrub sleeve smooths the towel. Warm, intimate, precise.

FORMAT (each image): ONE full-frame landscape photo, 3:2, 1536 x 1024 px, edge to edge. No borders, bands, blurred fill, collage, multiple panels or mock-ups.

STYLE: High-end editorial photography, true-to-life colours, natural skin, realistic – not illustration, 3D or CGI. Follow each scene exactly. Do NOT default to a white-and-gold luxury clinic, marble, orchids, beige knitwear or a doctor-patient desk scene, and do not reuse the same person in two images.

RULES: No text, letters, numbers, logos, labels, signage, readable screens or brand names anywhere. No before/after comparison, no results claims, no blood, wounds, incisions or needles in skin, no nudity or lingerie; modest clothing. No real or recognisable people or places. Also avoid: No needles, syringes, injection marks, pen markings on skin, mirrors, before/after, airbrushed plastic skin, long wavy hair, beige knitwear, marble or gold decor, wall panelling.
```


## Dental and Oral Health

### m745 — Prosthetic Dental Treatments

```
Create FOUR separate photorealistic photos for a premium German medical-travel website (MedLuxLife), topic "Prosthetic Dental Treatments" (Dental and Oral Health). Each image must be its OWN separate file, NOT a grid or collage, and the four images must show four clearly different ideas, people, settings, light and colours. Name them m745-1.png, m745-2.png, m745-3.png, m745-4.png.

IMAGE 1 – still life, no people: Still life, no people, outdoors on a sun-warmed sandstone garden wall. A three-unit tooth-coloured ceramic bridge rests on a smooth sand-coloured stone slab, with two single crowns just in front, one tilted to show its hollow inner shape. Beside them stands a small unglazed terracotta bowl. Low golden-hour sun from the side throws long warm shadows and makes the thin ceramic edges glow translucent ivory. Background: a terracotta plaster wall, softly blurred. Low eye-level angle, 100mm macro, crisp front crown.

IMAGE 2 – people: Side profile in a white-walled dental treatment room. A Gulf Arab woman in her 30s sits upright in a sage-green dental chair facing right: gold drop earrings, a white chiffon shayla loosely draped over dark hair. She listens with a calm, closed-lip smile. Facing her, also in profile, a dentist in his 60s, East Asian, cropped grey hair, round tortoiseshell glasses, sage scrubs, holds up a white plaster model of upper teeth with a small ceramic bridge. Hard noon sun casts crisp geometric window shadows across the wall.

IMAGE 3 – everyday life / another idea: Medium shot, waist up, of a German man in his 60s in his allotment garden at dawn. Tall and broad, short white hair, ruddy cheeks, a neat white moustache, rimless glasses; he wears a sage-green quilted vest over a white cotton shirt with rolled sleeves. Standing beneath an apple tree, he has just picked a crisp red apple and raises it with a relaxed grin, about to take a bite. Dew on the leaves, a white-painted wooden shed softly blurred behind. Cool, clear early-morning daylight, faint mist.

IMAGE 4 – process, detail or place: Symbolic architecture image: low angle from the ground looking up at a slender pale-grey concrete footbridge spanning the gap between two solid stone piers. Coral-painted steel railings run along its edge; far above, one anonymous walker crosses in tiny silhouette. The sky is bright white and luminous, high-key, the grey structure crisp and clean against it. Strong graphic diagonals, minimalist composition, 24mm wide lens. Light, airy, quietly engineered mood.

FORMAT (each image): ONE full-frame landscape photo, 3:2, 1536 x 1024 px, edge to edge. No borders, bands, blurred fill, collage, multiple panels or mock-ups.

STYLE: High-end editorial photography, true-to-life colours, natural skin, realistic – not illustration, 3D or CGI. Follow each scene exactly. Do NOT default to a white-and-gold luxury clinic, marble, orchids, beige knitwear or a doctor-patient desk scene, and do not reuse the same person in two images.

RULES: No text, letters, numbers, logos, labels, signage, readable screens or brand names anywhere. No before/after comparison, no results claims, no blood, wounds, incisions or needles in skin, no nudity or lingerie; modest clothing. No real or recognisable people or places. Also avoid: No open mouths or intraoral close-ups, no shade guides, mirrors, tablets or masks, no teeth on the footbridge, no text, numbers or signs.
```

### m743 — Implant-Supported Prostheses

```

Four slim titanium implant posts rise from the model; a full-arch tooth-coloured prosthesis rests just beside it, tilted slightly toward the camera. Dappled sunlight filtered through leaves falls across the scene in soft patches, catching a bright glint on the titanium. Three-quarter view, 100mm macro, shallow focus on the posts. Clean, technical, calm.

IMAGE 2 – people: Seen through a half-open glass door, its blurred frame edge in the foreground, a Kurdish couple in their 50s sit close together in a white recovery lounge at dusk. He has short wavy greying hair, a thick black-and-grey moustache and a white shirt, a marigold blanket over his knees, and holds a soft cold pack to his jaw with a calm half-smile. She has shoulder-length henna-auburn hair, small gold hoops and a marigold scarf, and rests a hand on his arm. Deep blue window light fills the room.

A Kurdish woman in her 60s sits at the lower edge of the frame, seen from above: thick silver hair in a short bob, a marigold cotton dress, gold bangles. Her hands tear a round flatbread over a spread of bowls: bulgur pilaf, whole walnuts, dried apricots, white yoghurt and sliced cucumber on a marigold cloth. Hard noon sun casts crisp shadows of a vine pergola across the table in sharp graphic lines. Generous, sunny, sociable mood.

IMAGE 4 – process, detail or place: Framed through the glass pane of a workshop door, its soft-focus wooden frame edging the left of the picture, a dental lab at evening. A technician is seen from behind, only shoulders and forearms in sand-coloured scrubs, bent over a sandstone-toned workbench. Under two warm articulated lamps, their hands seat a full-arch tooth-coloured prosthesis onto a terracotta-tinted gypsum model with implant posts. Shelves of unlabelled sand-coloured plaster models line the terracotta wall behind. Warm pools of lamplight, dark window, focused and quiet.

Beyond the ledge, an out-of-focus sky fades from pale blue to gold. 100mm macro at ledge height. Elegant, light, understated.

He wears a long-sleeved sand-coloured linen shirt and terracotta chinos. Behind him rises a sun-faded terracotta wall. Soft overcast light, no hard shadows. 85mm, clean profile against the wall. Easy, everyday.

IMAGE 4 – process, detail or place: Three-quarter back view of a tailor at a walnut workbench in a bright, white-walled atelier. Only his shoulder, the back of his grey head and his working arm are visible as he smooths a navy wool jacket sleeve over a narrow walnut sleeve board, a heavy plain iron resting nearby beside a pincushion and large steel shears. Bright high-key daylight floods in from tall windows, almost shadowless. 50mm. Meticulous, calm craft.

Soft morning light through sheer curtains, warm white walls, a glass of water on a small side table. Gentle, unhurried.

IMAGE 3 – everyday life / another idea: Close-medium shot at eye level in a home kitchen in the evening, with no doorway framing. A Korean woman in her mid-50s with a short grey bob, fine natural lines at her eyes and real skin texture, in a rust-copper cotton shirt with pushed-up sleeves, laughs mid-sentence toward someone off-frame while tipping chopped herbs from a wooden board into a copper pan. Olive-green glazed tiles behind her; a warm pendant lamp overhead lights her face and the rising steam. Lively, at ease.

IMAGE 4 – process, detail or place: 85mm close-up in a small rope workshop. A rope maker's weathered hands, burgundy work-shirt cuffs buttoned at the wrists, twist three loose strands of cream cotton fibre together: to the left the strands hang soft and separate, past the hands they lock into one firm, tight spiral rope. Dappled light through leaves outside an open door flickers across hands and fibres. Blurred cream plaster wall and a worn wooden bench behind. Patient, skilled, strong.
